## 📎 Attachment · skill_listing · 2026-08-13T06:51:57.245Z

```
- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch
```

```
namespace Terraria.GameContent.UI.Minimap;

public class MinimapFrameManager : SelectionHolder<MinimapFrame>
{
	protected override void Configuration_OnLoad(Preferences obj)
	{
		ActiveSelectionConfigKey = Main.Configuration.Get("MinimapFrame", "Default");
	}

	protected override void Configuration_Save(Preferences obj)
	{
		obj.Put("MinimapFrame", ActiveSelectionConfigKey);
	}

		CreateAndAdd("Golden", new Vector2(-10f, -10f), new Vector2(136f, 248f), new Vector2(96f, 248f), new Vector2(116f, 248f), mode);

		CreateAndAdd("Sticks", new Vector2(-10f, -10f), new Vector2(148f, 234f), new Vector2(200f, 234f), new Vector2(174f, 234f), mode);
		CreateAndAdd("StoneGold", new Vector2(-15f, -15f), new Vector2(220f, 244f), new Vector2(244f, 188f), new Vector2(244f, 216f), mode);
		CreateAndAdd("TwigLeaf", new Vector2(-20f, -20f), new Vector2(206f, 242f), new Vector2(162f, 242f), new Vector2(184f, 242f), mode);
		CreateAndAdd("Leaf", new Vector2(-20f, -20f), new Vector2(212f, 244f), new Vector2(168f, 246f), new Vector2(190f, 246f), mode);

	private void CreateAndAdd(string name, Vector2 frameOffset, Vector2 resetPosition, Vector2 zoomInPosition, Vector2 zoomOutPosition, AssetRequestMode mode)
	{
		//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		MinimapFrameTemplate minimapFrameTemplate = new MinimapFrameTemplate(name, frameOffset, resetPosition, zoomInPosition, zoomOutPosition);
		Options.Add(name, minimapFrameTemplate.CreateInstance(mode));
	}

	public void DrawTo(SpriteBatch spriteBatch, Vector2 position)
	{
		ActiveSelection.MinimapPosition = position;
		ActiveSelection.Update();
		ActiveSelection.DrawBackground(spriteBatch);
	}

	{

	}
```

```
69	  async set<K extends keyof OptionsData>(key: K, value: OptionsData[K]) {

72	    try {
73	      await kvSet(KEY, JSON.stringify(this.data));

75	  }
76

78	    for (const fn of this.listeners) fn(this.data);
79	  }
80	}
```

```
  /** 全屏地图：半透明背景 + 完整世界图 + 边框 + 操作提示；支持拖动/缩放 */
  drawFullMap(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, world: World, mouseX: number, mouseY: number, mouseDown: boolean, entities: Entity[] = []) {
    const fm = this.fullMap;
    const viewW = ctx.canvas.width, viewH = ctx.canvas.height;
    this._fmWorldW = world.w;
    this._fmWorldH = world.h;
    // 拖动平移：按下首帧只同步基准点不位移（防地图瞬移）
    // _mapOpenClick：打开地图的那次点击（Game 置位）不参与拖拽，松开后才允许拖
    if (mouseDown && !this._mapOpenClick) {
      if (!this.mapDragging) {
        this.mapDragging = true;
      } else {
        fm.panX += mouseX - this.lastMouse.x;
        fm.panY += mouseY - this.lastMouse.y;
        // 拖动改变 pan 后按当前鼠标重设锚点（缓动中的缩放继续稳定）
        const viewW = ctx.canvas.width, viewH = ctx.canvas.height;
        fm.anchorU = (mouseX - (viewW / 2 - (this._fmWorldW * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panX)) / fm.zoom;
        fm.anchorV = (mouseY - (viewH / 2 - (this._fmWorldH * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panY)) / fm.zoom;
        fm.anchorMX = mouseX;
        fm.anchorMY = mouseY;
      }
    } else {
      if (!mouseDown) this._mapOpenClick = false; // 松开后恢复正常拖拽
      this.mapDragging = false;
    }
    this.lastMouse = { x: mouseX, y: mouseY };
    // 画布尺寸按世界比例
    const mapW = world.w * fm.zoom;
    const mapH = world.h * fm.zoom;
    // 初始居中玩家（首次打开）
    if (fm.panX === 0 && fm.panY === 0 && !this._mapInit) {
      this._mapInit = true;
      fm.panX = 0;
      fm.panY = 0;
    }
    const cx0 = viewW / 2 - mapW / 2 + fm.panX;
    const cy0 = viewH / 2 - mapH / 2 + fm.panY;
    // 背景：原版 DrawMapFullscreenBackground（Main.cs:55804）——全屏群系 MapBG 贴图
    // （TextureAssets.MapBGs[i] = MapBG{i+1}.png，AssetInitializer :641），按视区深度
    // 与玩家 zone 选表；贴图 vui() 懒加载首帧 null → 次帧补上（兜底深色）
    const bgTex = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vui(`MapBG${this.fullMapBgIndex(world) + 1}`) : null;
    if (bgTex) {
      ctx.drawImage(bgTex.img, bgTex.sx, bgTex.sy, bgTex.sw, bgTex.sh, 0, 0, viewW, viewH);
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,16,0.92)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewW, viewH);
    }
    // 卷轴底图（原版 Images/Map.png 928×248 羊皮纸，Main.cs:54923：地图内容【之下】
    // 整幅绘制——destRect 与地图区同位同缩放，羊皮纸边缘饰纹在地图缩放留边处露出；
    // 地图探索未达区域在卷轴上呈现纸张色而非纯蓝）
    const scroll = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vui('Map') : null;
    if (scroll && scroll.img instanceof HTMLImageElement && scroll.img.complete && scroll.img.naturalWidth > 0) {
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; // 卷轴是美术图，平滑缩放防像素锯齿
      ctx.drawImage(scroll.img, scroll.sx, scroll.sy, scroll.sw, scroll.sh, cx0, cy0, mapW, mapH);
    }
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = fm.zoom >= 1;
    ctx.drawImage(this.minimap!.canvas, cx0, cy0, mapW, mapH);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    // 迷雾
    this.drawFog(ctx, world, cx0, cy0, fm.zoom, 2 * fm.zoom);
    // 头像层(原版 DrawNPCMapIcons2 :55602 + MapPlayerRenderer:玩家头/城镇 NPC 头/
    // Boss 头;headScale=min(1,(zoom×0.5+1)/3)——Main.cs:55140)
    const p = this._lastPlayer;
    if (p) {
      this.drawMapHeads(ctx, p, Math.min(1, (fm.zoom * 0.2 * 2 + 1) / 3) * 1.1,
        (wx) => cx0 + wx / TILE * fm.zoom,
        (wy) => cy0 + wy / TILE * fm.zoom,
        entities.filter((e) => e instanceof TownNPC) as TownNPC[],
        entities.filter((e) => e instanceof Enemy) as Enemy[]);
    }
    // 传送预选标记（第一次点击的点，闪烁提示再次点击确认）
    const tp = this.tpMark;
    if (tp) {
      const blink = 0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.008);
      ctx.globalAlpha = blink;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#FF5050';
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      const mx = cx0 + (tp.x + 0.5) * fm.zoom, my = cy0 + (tp.y + 0.5) * fm.zoom;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(mx, my, Math.max(8, 10 * fm.zoom), 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(mx - 14, my); ctx.lineTo(mx + 14, my);
      ctx.moveTo(mx, my - 14); ctx.lineTo(mx, my + 14);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    // 操作提示
    this.drawPylonMapLayer(ctx, cx0, cy0, mouseX, mouseY);
    ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#C8C0D8';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Map.Hint'), viewW / 2, viewH - 16);
  }
  private _mapInit = false;

  /** 全屏地图晶塔图标层（TeleportPylonsMapLayer.cs 1:1）：
   *   - 连线（:52-75）：当前塔（MapPylonTile）→ 其余每座晶塔的黑 6px/白 2px 双线
   *   - 图标（:77-104）：Extra_182.png 11 帧横排（330×38，帧 30×38），帧号 = 塔型；
   *     玩家不在晶塔旁 → 半透明灰（color = Gray*0.5 :70-73）
   *   - 悬停提示（:97-99）：晶塔物品名
   *  击中判定（Game.tryPylonMapTeleport）：图标中心 = 锚点 + (1.5, 2) 格。 */
  private drawPylonMapLayer(
    ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx0: number, cy0: number,
    mouseX: number, mouseY: number,
  ) {
    if (!this.pylons.length) { this.pylonHoverName = null; return; }
    const fm = this.fullMap;
    const icon = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vui('Extra_182.png') : null;
    // 玩家 ±60 格内无晶塔 → 半透明（IsPlayerNearAPylon :70-73；近似取相机中心玩家位）
    const p = this._lastPlayer;
    const dim = p ? !this.pylons.some((py) =>
      Math.abs(py.x - p.cx / TILE) <= 62 && Math.abs(py.y - p.cy / TILE) <= 63) : true;
    // 连线（:52-75）
    if (this.mapPylonTile) {
      const o = this.mapPylonTile;
      const ox = cx0 + (o.x + 1.5) * fm.zoom, oy = cy0 + (o.y + 2) * fm.zoom;
      for (const py of this.pylons) {
        if (py.x === o.x && py.y === o.y) continue;
        const tx = cx0 + (py.x + 1.5) * fm.zoom, ty = cy0 + (py.y + 2) * fm.zoom;
        for (const [c, w] of [['#000000', 6], ['#FFFFFF', 2]] as const) {
          ctx.strokeStyle = c;
          ctx.lineWidth = w;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ox, oy); ctx.lineTo(tx, ty); ctx.stroke();
        }
      }
    }
    // 图标 + 悬停
    this.pylonHoverName = null;
    const FW = 30, FH = 38;   // Extra_182：11 帧 × 30px（330×38）
    const scale = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, fm.zoom));   // scaleIfSelected 2× / 基线 1×
    for (const py of this.pylons) {
      const mx = cx0 + (py.x + 1.5) * fm.zoom, my = cy0 + (py.y + 2) * fm.zoom;
      const dw = FW * scale, dh = FH * scale;
      if (mx < -dw || mx > ctx.canvas.width + dw || my < -dh || my > ctx.canvas.height + dh) continue;
      ctx.globalAlpha = dim ? 0.35 : 1;
      if (icon) {
        ctx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx + py.type * FW, icon.sy, FW, FH,
          mx - dw / 2, my - dh / 2, dw, dh);
      } else {
        // 贴图未就绪兜底：白描边菱形占位（懒加载次帧补上真图标）
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#8CF5A9'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(mx, my - dh / 2); ctx.lineTo(mx + dw / 2, my);
        ctx.lineTo(mx, my + dh / 2); ctx.lineTo(mx - dw / 2, my);
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (Math.abs(mouseX - mx) <= dw / 2 && Math.abs(mouseY - my) <= dh / 2) {
        this.pylonHoverName = Lang.itemName(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(py.type));
      }
    }
    if (this.pylonHoverName) {
      ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText(this.pylonHoverName, mouseX, mouseY - 14);
    }
  }

  /** 全屏地图背景群系序号（DrawMapFullscreenBackground Main.cs:55804-55905 1:1）。

    const vy = Math.floor((cy0 + mapH / 2) / fm.zoom);

    const inB = st.inBounds(px, py);
    const wall = inB ? st.wall[st.idx(px, py)] : 0;
    // 地狱（视区深度）
    if (vy > world.store.h - 232) return 2;
    if (sc.zoneDungeon) return 4;
    if (wall === 87) return 13;
    if (py > world.groundLevel) {
      switch (wall) {
        case 86: case 108: return 15;
        case 180: case 184: return 16;
        case 178: case 183: return 17;
```

```
30	export class MenuBackground {
31	  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
32	  private ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
33	  private sky = new SkyRenderer();
34	  /** 菜单时钟：一圈 2.5 分钟（原版 30min 太慢，日月升降肉眼不可见） */
35	  private clock = new Clock(24 * 60 * 1000); // 与世界同周期 24min（1:1）

39	  private variantT = 0;
40	
41	  constructor(parent: HTMLElement) {
42	    this.canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
43	    this.canvas.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:0;';
44	    parent.insertBefore(this.canvas, parent.firstChild);
45	    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d')!;
46	    this.resize();
47	    window.addEventListener('resize', this.resize);
48	  }
49	
50	  private resize = () => {
51	    this.canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
52	    this.canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
53	  };
54	
55	  private img(n: number): HTMLImageElement | null {
56	    if (n < 0) return null;
57	    if (this.imgs.has(n)) return this.imgs.get(n) ?? null;
58	    const im = new Image();
59	    im.src = `sprites/vanilla/Background_${n}.png`;
60	    this.imgs.set(n, im);
61	    return im;
62	  }
63	
64	  /** UI 像素 → 当前屏幕像素（原版 UIScale 语义） */
65	  private ui(): number { return this.canvas.height / UI_H; }
66	
67	  /** 单层横滚平铺（原版 DrawSurfaceBG 主循环） */
68	  private drawLayer(n: number, parallax: number, y: number, scale: number, alpha = 1) {
69	    const im = this.img(n);
70	    if (!im || !im.complete || im.naturalWidth === 0 || alpha <= 0.01) return;
71	    const w = im.naturalWidth * scale, h = im.naturalHeight * scale;
72	    const startX = -(((this.camX * parallax) % w) + w) % w - w / 2;
73	    const loops = Math.ceil(this.canvas.width / w) + 2;
74	    const ctx = this.ctx;
75	    ctx.save();
76	    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
77	    // 接缝修复：浮点 X/宽度平铺会在相邻贴图间露出亚像素缝隙——
78	    // 坐标取整 + 每张多画 1px 重叠（背景层无感知，消除滚动接缝）
79	    const yR = Math.round(y);
80	    for (let i = 0; i < loops; i++) {
81	      ctx.drawImage(im, Math.round(startX + i * w), yR, Math.ceil(w) + 1, h);
82	    }
83	    ctx.restore();
84	  }
85	
86	  /** 画一套森林变体（绝对 UI 坐标 + 贴图特例偏移，:60815 起） */
87	  private drawVariant(idx: number, alpha: number) {
88	    const v = FOREST_VARIANTS[idx % FOREST_VARIANTS.length];
89	    const u = this.ui();
90	    // 远山（:59257 y=100 / :59307 y=230；parallax 0.1/0.18，scale 1）
91	    this.drawLayer(v.mnt[0], 0.10, 100 * u, u, alpha);
92	    this.drawLayer(v.mnt[1], 0.18, 230 * u, u, alpha);
93	    // 树层（gameMenu: y=320/400/480；scale 1.25；parallax 0.4）
94	    const treeYBase = [320, 400, 480];
95	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
96	      const tex = v.trees[i];
97	      if (tex < 0) continue;
98	      let y = treeYBase[i];
99	      // 贴图特例（:60815-60854：50→-50、53→-100、91/173→+200、178→+75、52→-50、55→-100、92→+150）
100	      if (tex === 50 || tex === 51 || tex === 52) y -= 50;
101	      else if (tex === 53 || tex === 54 || tex === 55) y -= 100;
102	      else if (tex === 91 || tex === 92 || tex === 173) y += 150 + (i === 0 ? 50 : 0);
103	      else if (tex === 178) y += 75;
104	      this.drawLayer(tex, 0.4, y * u, 1.25 * u, alpha);
105	    }
106	  }
107	
108	  tick(dtMs: number) {
109	    this.clock.tick(dtMs);
110	    this.camX += 4 * (dtMs / 16.7); // 原版 MenuXMovement(4f)/帧
111	    const W = this.canvas.width, H = this.canvas.height;
112	    menuTimeInfo.timeOfDay = this.clock.timeOfDay;
113	    menuTimeInfo.dayFactor = this.clock.dayFactor;
114	
115	    this.sky.draw(this.ctx, this.clock, W, H, this.camX, dtMs);
116	
117	    // 森林变体轮换 + 交叉淡化（bgAlpha 语义）
118	    this.variantT += dtMs;
119	    const cycle = VARIANT_HOLD_MS + VARIANT_FADE_MS;
120	    if (this.variantT >= cycle) {
121	      this.variantT -= cycle;
122	      this.variantIdx = (this.variantIdx + 1) % FOREST_VARIANTS.length;
123	    }
124	    const fade = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (this.variantT - VARIANT_HOLD_MS) / VARIANT_FADE_MS));
125	    this.drawVariant(this.variantIdx, 1 - fade);
126	    if (fade > 0) this.drawVariant(this.variantIdx + 1, fade);
127	
128	    // 夜间压暗（ColorOfSurfaceBackgrounds 随昼夜变暗；只压背景不压 DOM UI）
129	    const dark = (1 - this.clock.dayFactor) * 0.72;
130	    if (dark > 0.01) {
131	      this.ctx.save();
132	      this.ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
133	      this.ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(255 - dark * 170)},${Math.round(255 - dark * 150)},${Math.round(255 - dark * 60)})`;
134	      this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
135	      this.ctx.restore();
136	    }
137	  }
138	
139	  destroy() {
140	    window.removeEventListener('resize', this.resize);
141	    this.canvas.remove();
142	  }
143	
144	  /** 调试探针 */
145	  lastDt = 0;
146	  clockTime(): number { return this.clock.timeOfDay; }
147	}
```

```
}
`;
```

```
1	// 键值存储：优先 IndexedDB（容量数百 MB+，大地图存档不受 localStorage ~5MB 限制），
2	// localStorage 作为小存档缓存与回退。async API，调用方 await。
3	const DB_NAME = 'sandboxworld';
4	const STORE = 'kv';
5	let dbPromise: Promise<IDBDatabase> | null = null;
6	
7	function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
8	  if (dbPromise) return dbPromise;
9	  dbPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
10	    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1);
11	    req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
12	      if (!req.result.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE)) req.result.createObjectStore(STORE);
13	    };
14	    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
15	    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
16	  });
17	  return dbPromise;
18	}
19	
20	/** 是否有某 key（IndexedDB 或 localStorage） */
21	export async function kvHas(key: string): Promise<boolean> {
22	  if (localStorage.getItem(key) !== null) return true;
23	  try {
24	    const db = await openDb();
25	    return await new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
26	      const tx = db.transaction(STORE, 'readonly');
27	      const req = tx.objectStore(STORE).getKey(key);
28	      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result !== undefined);
29	      req.onerror = () => resolve(false);
30	    });
31	  } catch {
32	    return false;
33	  }
34	}
35	
36	/** 只读 IndexedDB（worker 安全：无 localStorage 依赖；大存档只进 IDB）。
37	 *  命中返回字符串，未命中/不可用返回 null */
38	export async function kvGetIdb(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
39	  try {
40	    const db = await openDb();
41	    return await new Promise<string | null>((resolve) => {
42	      const tx = db.transaction(STORE, 'readonly');
43	      const req = tx.objectStore(STORE).get(key);
44	      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(typeof req.result === 'string' ? req.result : null);
45	      req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
46	    });
47	  } catch {
48	    return null;
49	  }
50	}
51	
52	/** 读取（IndexedDB 优先——大存档只进 IDB，localStorage 可能留着旧的小存档；
53	   读取顺序必须与写入优先级一致，否则会读到过期档） */
54	export async function kvGet(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
55	  const idbVal = await kvGetIdb(key);
56	  if (idbVal !== null) return idbVal;
57	  return localStorage.getItem(key);
58	}
59	
60	/** 写入：字符串 ≤ 2MB 进 localStorage（快速路径），否则只进 IndexedDB */
61	export async function kvSet(key: string, value: string): Promise<'local' | 'idb' | 'fail'> {
62	  if (value.length <= 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
63	    try {
64	      localStorage.setItem(key, value);
65	      return 'local';
66	    } catch { /* 超限或被禁 → 落 IndexedDB */ }
67	  }
68	  try {
69	    const db = await openDb();
70	    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
71	      const tx = db.transaction(STORE, 'readwrite');
72	      tx.objectStore(STORE).put(value, key);
73	      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
74	      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
75	    });
76	    return 'idb';
77	  } catch {
78	    return 'fail';
79	  }
80	}
81	
82	/** 删除（两处都清） */
83	export async function kvDelete(key: string): Promise<void> {
84	  localStorage.removeItem(key);
85	  try {
86	    const db = await openDb();
87	    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
88	      const tx = db.transaction(STORE, 'readwrite');
89	      tx.objectStore(STORE).delete(key);
90	      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
91	      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
92	    });
93	  } catch { /* IDB 不可用则忽略 */ }
94	}
95
```

````
Now I have the complete picture. Current state:

**Part 1 (fullscreen map skins)**: 
- The premise is false. Vanilla 1.4.5.6 has NO fullscreen map skin system:
  - `AssetInitializer.cs:636-641`: MapDeath, Map (single texture), MapBG1-42. No skin array.
  - `MinimapFrameManager.cs` (Terraria.GameContent.UI.Minimap): exactly 9 skins.
  - `ResourcePacksDefaultInfo.tsv:14756`: only `Images/UI/MapPing` exists under UI/Map*; no `Images/UI/Map/` dir.
  - `en-US.json:212-221`: `SelectMapBorder` = "Minimap Border: {0}" + 9 `MinimapFrame_*` keys; no `MapSkin_*` keys.
  - Asset dump has no UI/Map/ dir and no UI_Map_* files.
- The repo already implements:
  - Fullscreen map with vanilla parchment (`Map.png` 928×248) at Renderer.ts:5318-5325, MapBG biome backgrounds (fullMapBgIndex), fog, heads, pylon layer.
  - Minimap frame skin system fully: Options.minimapFrame (9 skins), Settings row (ui category), Renderer.MINIMAP_SKINS + texture loading + drawing.

So for part 1, there is nothing vanilla-valid to add. The correct action: report. But the parent asked for a test file. I'll deliver `tests/map-skins.test.ts` that locks in the existing real feature (guarding against regression):
- 9 skins × 4 texture files exist in public/sprites/vanilla-ui (fs check) — and dist? No, public only.
- Options default minimapFrame = 'Default', persistence round-trip.
- Pure cycle function.

To make the cycle testable and shared (single source of truth), I'll add to Options.ts:
```ts
export const MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS = ['Default','Golden','Remix','Sticks','StoneGold','TwigLeaf','Leaf','Retro','Valkyrie'] as const;
export type MinimapFrameSkin = typeof MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS[number];
export function cycleMinimapFrame(current: string): MinimapFrameSkin
````

```
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using ReLogic.Content;
using Terraria.IO;

namespace Terraria.DataStructures;

public abstract class SelectionHolder<TCycleType> where TCycleType : class, IConfigKeyHolder
{
	protected Dictionary<string, TCycleType> Options = new Dictionary<string, TCycleType>();

	protected TCycleType ActiveSelection;

	protected string ActiveSelectionConfigKey;

	protected bool LoadedContent;

	public string ActiveSelectionKeyName { get; private set; }

	public void BindTo(Preferences preferences)
	{
		preferences.OnLoad += Wrapped_Configuration_OnLoad;
		preferences.OnSave += Configuration_Save;
	}

	protected abstract void Configuration_Save(Preferences obj);

	protected abstract void Configuration_OnLoad(Preferences obj);

	protected void Wrapped_Configuration_OnLoad(Preferences obj)
	{
		Configuration_OnLoad(obj);
		if (LoadedContent)
		{
			SetActiveMinimapFromLoadedConfigKey();
		}
	}

	protected abstract void PopulateOptionsAndLoadContent(AssetRequestMode mode);

	public void LoadContent(AssetRequestMode mode)
	{
		//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		PopulateOptionsAndLoadContent(mode);
		LoadedContent = true;
		SetActiveMinimapFromLoadedConfigKey();
	}

	public void CycleSelection()
	{
		TCycleType lastFrame = null;
		Options.Values.FirstOrDefault(delegate(TCycleType frame)
		{
			if (frame == ActiveSelection)
			{
				return true;
			}
			lastFrame = frame;
			return false;
		});
		if (lastFrame == null)
		{
			lastFrame = Options.Values.Last();
		}
		SetActiveFrame(lastFrame);
	}

	public void SetActiveMinimapFromLoadedConfigKey()
	{
		SetActiveFrame(ActiveSelectionConfigKey);
	}

	private void SetActiveFrame(string frameName)
	{
		TCycleType val = Options.FirstOrDefault((KeyValuePair<string, TCycleType> pair) => pair.Key == frameName).Value;
		if (val == null)
		{
			val = Options.Values.First();
		}
		SetActiveFrame(val);
	}

	private void SetActiveFrame(TCycleType frame)
	{
		ActiveSelection = frame;
		ActiveSelectionConfigKey = frame.ConfigKey;
		ActiveSelectionKeyName = frame.NameKey;
	}
}

```

````
Note: CycleSelection cycles... interesting: it finds the element BEFORE the active one in the dictionary (lastFrame = previous), or Last() if active is first. export function cycleMinimapFrame(current: string): MinimapFrameSkin {

  if (i === -1) return 'Default'; // SetActiveFrame unknown → Options.Values.First()
  return MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS[(i - 1 + N) % N];
}
````

````
export const MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS = ['Default', 'Golden', 'Remix', 'Sticks', 'StoneGold', 'TwigLeaf', 'Leaf', 'Retro', 'Valkyrie'] as const;
export type MinimapFrameSkin = typeof MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS[number];

/** 皮肤循环（SelectionHolder.CycleSelection 1:1：Dictionary 枚举序中取 ActiveSelection 的
 *  【前一个】，ActiveSelection 是首个时回绕到末个；未知键先经 SetActiveFrame 落首个 Default） */
export function cycleMinimapFrame(current: string): MinimapFrameSkin {
  const i = MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS.indexOf(current as MinimapFrameSkin);
  if (i === -1) return MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS[0]; // SetActiveFrame(string) 未命中 → Options.Values.First()
  return MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS[(i - 1 + MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS.length) % MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS.length];
}
```

```ts
describe('小地图边框皮肤（MinimapFrameManager）', () => {
  it('9 款皮肤 = 原版插入序（MinimapFrameManager.cs:32-42）', () => {
    expect([...MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS]).toEqual(['Default','Golden','Remix','Sticks','StoneGold','TwigLeaf','Leaf','Retro','Valkyrie']);
  });
  it('36 张皮肤贴图全部存在', () => {
    const parts = ['MinimapFrame','MinimapButton_Reset','MinimapButton_ZoomIn','MinimapButton_ZoomOut'];
    for (const s of MINIMAP_FRAME_SKINS) for (const p of parts) {
      const f = resolve(__dirname, `../public/sprites/vanilla-ui/UI_Minimap_${s}_${p}.png`);
      expect(existsSync(f), f).toBe(true);
    }
  });
  it('切换纯函数 = CycleSelection 回绕（前一个,首→末）', () => {
    expect(cycleMinimapFrame('Default')).toBe('Valkyrie');
    expect(cycleMinimapFrame('Golden')).toBe('Default');
    expect(cycleMinimapFrame('Valkyrie')).toBe('Retro');
    expect(cycleMinimapFrame('bogus')).toBe('Default');
    // 全环回绕一轮回到自身
    let cur = 'Default'; const seen = [];
    for (let i=0;i<9;i++){ cur = cycleMinimapFrame(cur); seen.push(cur); }
    expect(seen).toHaveLength(9);
    expect(new Set(seen).size).toBe(9);
    expect(cycleMinimapFrame(cur)).toBe('Default');
  });
  it('Options 持久化:默认 Default,set 落 kv,新实例回读', async () => {
    const a = new OptionsStore();
    await a.load();
    expect(a.data.minimapFrame).toBe('Default');
    await a.set('minimapFrame', 'Golden');
    const b = new OptionsStore();
    await b.load();
    expect(b.data.minimapFrame).toBe('Golden');
  });
  it('未知存档键归一化 → Default（SelectionHolder.SetActiveFrame 兜底）', async () => {
    mem.set('sandboxworld.options', JSON.stringify({ minimapFrame: 'Nope' }));
    const c = new OptionsStore();
    await c.load();
    expect(c.data.minimapFrame).toBe('Default');
  });
});
````

```json
{
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tests/splash-screens.test.ts",
 "content": "// SplashScreens 素材加载确认（任务 2 的\"3 行加载测试\"落点）。\n// 原版用法（AssetInitializer.cs:81-92 / Main.cs:51908+ DrawSplash）——这些是【启动加载\n// 画面】素材，不是主菜单背景轮播：\n//   - Splash_1            → SplashTexture16x9（BlackFadeIn 淡入贴图，Main.cs:61869）\n//   - Splash_{1..10}_{0,1,2} → LegoBack/LegoTree/LegoFront 三件套，每次启动\n//                             UnifiedRandom.Next(1,11) 随机选一组（:86-89）\n//   - ResonanceArray      → SplashTextureLegoResonanace（:85）\n//   - Splash_0            → 原版代码零引用（全仓 grep 仅 tsv 列表），仅素材在包\n// 主菜单背景 = 森林视差（DrawSurfaceBG_Forest，仓库 MenuBackground.ts 1:1），不用这些图。\n// 启动画面本体（DrawSplash 渐隐时序 + LoadingSunflower）未实装 → 登记项，本测试只锁素材可用。\nimport { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\nimport { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n\n/** PNG IHDR 宽高（字节 16/20，big-endian）——无解码器的\"可加载\"校验 */\nfunction pngSize(f: string): [number, number] {\n  const d = readFileSync(f);\n  expect(d.subarray(1, 4).toString('ascii'), f).toBe('PNG');\n  return [d.readUInt32BE(16), d.readUInt32BE(20)];\n}\n\ndescribe('SplashScreens 素材可加载', () => {\n  it('原版引用的 32 张全部存在且为有效 PNG（Splash_1 + ResonanceArray + 10 组三件套）', () => {\n    const names = ['Splash_1', 'ResonanceArray'];\n    for (let n = 1; n <= 10; n++) for (let p = 0; p < 3; p++) names.push(`Splash_${n}_${p}`);\n    expect(names).toHaveLength(32);\n    for (const n of names) {\n      const f = `public/sprites/vanilla/${n}.png`;\n      expect(existsSync(f), f).toBe(true);\n      const [w, h] = pngSize(f);\n      expect(w, f).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n      expect(h, f).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('随机三件套同尺寸（DrawSplash :51997-52024 按 Back 尺寸等比铺满，组内混搭会露底）', () => {\n    for (let n = 1; n <= 10; n++) {\n      const [w0, h0] = pngSize(`public/sprites/vanilla/Splash_${n}_0.png`);\n      for (let p = 1; p < 3; p++) {\n        const [w, h] = pngSize(`public/sprites/vanilla/Splash_${n}_${p}.png`);\n        expect([w, h], `Splash_${n}_${p}`).toEqual([w0, h0]);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n});\n"
}
```
